## §1 · Mental models

- **(S325) A void closes everything reachable from the record (F-922).** A voided case still had papers with their own links; a list that hides a row is not a door that is shut. The walk asks for every file of a voided record by its own address.
- **(S325) A walk on a copy of live data finds its own rows by a mark it wrote (F-923).** "The first row" on the live box was a test's removed row. The installer walking the live copy is what caught it — that gate stays.
- **(S325) A file time is not proof of the bytes (F-924).** A commit to the PC from a reused staged path can land an older upload with a new time. Fresh staged path every time; read back by hash before naming the publish.
- **(S325) The live test is a dummy record walked by a sub-agent as the owner, then removed (his word 12:17).** Every step, every refusal, every removal — and what it finds is a fix kit, not a note.
- **(S319) An outage is read from the machine's own record, and the record must survive the restart (F-919).** `last -x` gives the shutdown and the reboot; `/var/log/messages` named the hypervisor. A journal that is not persistent forgets exactly the minutes that matter.
- **(S319) An alert that matters goes by two roads, and the road through a shared free service is retried until it is taken (F-917).** ntfy.sh limits each address, and Google's addresses are shared by everyone's scripts.
- **(S319) Docterz is the record for a name; a copy that keeps first-seen values is corrected by the newest record (F-918, D733).** A one-off data fix to `patient_ref` is overwritten by the next sync — mend it where it is read, or at its source.

- **A job is watched by what it produces, never by whether its process is up (F-894).** `wa-notifier` was *active* for six days while it sent nothing. Any relay that waits on Google gets a timeout and a loud failure.
- **The API credits pay for the Agent SDK, not Claude Code (F-895).** Until 17-Oct a build is a brief in `claude_code_briefs\` plus the owner's one line: `python D:\Downloads\_kbtools\agent_build\run_build.py <BRIEF>`. The runner builds, walks, installs, publishes and writes `REPORT_<kit>.md`. It cannot write the board, so the chat adds its lines.
- **A link is a person's word, kept with who and when (D726).** Where the system cannot know two numbers are one patient, a staff tap says so, can be undone the same day, and is never guessed.


- **(S311) A source is chosen by whether it covers the output's day, not by being newest (F-884).** And a list built short is a state the code heals the next time it runs.
- **(S311) A line for the owner is marked as his where it is made (F-885)** — then every road out of the page (mail, checklist, export) is walked for it. What he wants kept aside goes to one place he sees, and to nobody else until he says (D717).
- **(S311) A split bill is shown as it was paid (D718):** Docterz gives the parts per bill; matched to the items they fall out one-to-one in most bills, by grouping in a few, and only rarely as one item in two parts — the totals are exact in all. Net and match at the bill, never the day (F-886).

**Added at S305:**

- **(S305) A gap between two figures is explained from the rows behind both, never from their difference (F-870).** The owner asks for the test in one line; run it before explaining.
- **(S305) Docterz is the proof of an online booking (D714):** the Razorpay number sits on the consultation line; a *Wallet* entry without it is a reception error, decided against the bank. Split payments carry their parts on the line — the pack must use them.
- **(S305) A phone feature is proved on a phone (F-871).** An emulated browser proves a link's shape, not what Chrome on Android will do with it; a permanent redirect lives in a phone's cache for ever — change a forward with a temporary one.

**Added at S304 (the lists below are v222's, v220's, v218's, v216's and v215's, whole):**

- **A correction is part of the build** (D704, `BUILD_NORM_v1`): the code heals the data it governs, by itself, every run — a reader that learns a layout re-offers what it refused; new columns are filled from the files already kept; a patient's words are scrubbed on every nightly read. A one-off is the exception and rides the installer.
- **A sheet a script writes decides by what a message IS, not who sent it** (F-834). A sender list lets in OTPs, failures and a patient's name.
- **A job a person ran once is not a pipeline** (F-835): the card statements were decrypted by a hand-run script, so October simply never came.
- **An alert day and a deadline are two fields** (F-775): count to the expiry; the alert day only decides when to start.
- **Every kit number is claimed on the board, not only the first of an evening** (F-831); **a `.gitignore` check is last-match-wins** (F-832).

**Added at S302 (the lists below are v220's, v218's, v216's and v215's, whole):**

- **A backup is a backup when it has been restored once.** Two files with the same purpose and different contents (34 members against 29) are two different things until a restore says otherwise; until then the older habit stays.
- **An alarm's threshold comes from the rule it watches.** The prune rule allowed twelve; the alarm typed six beside it and cried wolf for two weeks (F-806).
- **A no-show is judged only on data that reaches the day.** The visit export arrives in the evening; a list that judged at noon would have called yesterday's patient absent (F-811, D696).

**Added at S300 (the lists below are v218's, v216's and v215's, whole):**

- **One fact, one function.** Whether a POS figure stands in for the bank is asked in one place — `bank_standin.standing(con, unit, day)` — by every unit's reconciler; a second copy of that question in another file is how two screens come to disagree. And **a function named as a read does not create, sweep or commit** (F-793): the table is made and old rows are closed on a page read, never inside a reconciler.
- **A stand-in is never the record.** A typed figure changes nothing until he confirms it; confirmed, it marks the day *provisional*; the bank's own row is read first, so the bank replaces it without anyone acting; the typed row is kept as what was said, with the difference beside it. No money row is written by any of it.
- **A time in a record is read, not felt** (F-791). The clock tool, a commit stamp, a file's time, a program's own line — or the sentence carries no time.

**Added at S298 (the lists below are v216's and v215's, whole):**

- **A floor is tested for what it ADDS as well as what it hides** (F-780). A view over a table that another duty subtracts from — *"is it done?"* — hides the finished rows and makes work appear. Such a table is not shadowed; the duty that needs the floor is rewritten in its own SQL (`aaj_seed.REWRITE`), and a count the floor produced out of nothing is capped.
- **A switch that cannot read its own state is off.** The lists fail closed: an unreadable panel switches every list off, a damaged document that person's; the page helper answers *no floor* on any error, so a page is never broken by it.
- **The kit that is published is the kit that is walked** — from where it is packed, once through a linked path (F-776), and in the server's time zone as well as the build machine's (F-772).
- **A panel that saves itself is looked at by reads only.** Every change on `/finance/aaj` is saved at once; the assistant's own look, and a sub-agent's, presses nothing and posts nothing.

**Added at S297 (the list below is v215's, whole):**

- **A page that must agree with a store is worked back from that store.** The private loan page takes its opening from the ledger on the box and a record that holds months and kinds only; it cannot disagree with the ledger, and the kit holds no person's figure (F-757).
- **On an append-only store the undo is an append** (F-758). A correction is one new row with a tag, a dated backup and a note; it is taken back by a second row.
- **A preview of money pages says which settings it was worked on, and the live page is read again just before he is asked to lock** (F-761). **A sentence printed for staff is a claim about the system — read it in the code first** (F-760).
- **His approvals are few and arrive together** (D683): finish everything that does not prompt, then ask once. **What he types on his board is answered at the next close, on the board** (D684).

1. **A reading layer computes nothing.** The console asks each owning module on a COPY of the database (`FINANCE_DB` pointed at the copy before any module loads) and writes one file; the service only reads the file. A new figure for the owner goes into the module that owns it, then into the console — never the other way.
2. **One list engine, two readers.** `aaj_kaam.build_all` feeds the staff's page (live, through a `mode=ro` connection) and the console's builder (on the copy). A change to a duty's wording or count is made once — in `DUTY_MAP.json` (the Sanjeevni chat's) or `aaj_duties.json` (the parent's) — and both screens follow.
3. **The floor is laid under the SQL, not written into it.** TEMP views named like the floored tables on the engine's own connection; the map's SQL runs unedited. A `main.<table>` or a `rowid` in a `due_sql` steps around it (the walk's check A3c is red on that).
4. **A list that is not whole says so** (F-750) — and **a guarded piece of a reading says it could not be read; it is never simply absent** (F-748).
5. **A walk is run on data shaped unlike the box it was written on, and on more than one clock** (F-746); **a negative control that survives is a finding about the check** (F-747).
6. **The login gate answers before routing** — a door answering 302 without a login proves nothing about whether it is mounted. The proof is to load the placed app on a copy and ask the app for its routes (`reading_s487.py mounted`).
7. **A figure for the owner is read where he will read it** (F-724).
8. **A database copy is made only under the session's own home; a scratch box is hashed against the live pins before the first walk** (F-752). **After a kit is placed, `.gitignore` is read against every file of it before the publish is named** (F-751).
9. **When he stops a build, it stops at once and he is shown what he asked about** — the model question of 05-Oct: paused, the session's setting shown, resumed at his word.
10. Carried from v212: a page tells you where to look, the store tells you what is true · a refused file is retried when its reader changes · an export of a program's configuration is read for every field (F-721) · a job under someone's Google authorisation is watched by what it produces (F-723) · a store that staff can read is not a road for a bank statement (D674) · *"take my yes"* — once he has corrected the plan, build the whole plan · two chats, one working copy, one publish: a file the other chat owns is read live before it is touched · the board's one counter costs a full rewrite per line: trim before 200 KB (F-703) · a walk is hermetic by construction (F-709) · a rule is walked with every value it names (F-710) · the gate is run as PUBLISH runs it · read `whoami` before an owner-only press · what his word closed is not raised again · the build lock binds every installer (F-694) · patient files never pass through the assistant's workspace (D656) · a system change on a PC is his hand.

## §1A · How to send a job to the reception PC

**Added at S302:** job j44 (08-Oct 20:41–20:42 IST) put back the desktop's `X-ray upload` shortcut — Windows Explorer as the target, the X-ray inbox folder as its argument — so Windows' clean-up of broken shortcuts leaves it alone (F-809). The next job number is **j45** (the old DriveFS folder, 08:30 on a clinic day). The signing key stays on manojz in `margsync\_config\` and is never printed; a job is committed first, then its `.sig`.

**Sign (both doors), in manojz's own shell — the secret never leaves that PC:**

```
cd <folder with the job> && python3 -B $HOME/mnt/dr-manoj-git/drmanoj-clinic-automation/deploy_kits/S448_RECEPTION_AGENT/reception_sign.py sign $HOME/mnt/Downloads/margsync/_config/reception_agent_key.txt <job file>
```

It writes `<IST time>_<job>` and `<IST time>_<job>.sig`. Job folders so far: `D:\Downloads\ClaudeCowork\03_WORKING_PAPERS\_EVIDENCE\S290\reception_jobs\` (j01 … j21) and `…\_EVIDENCE\S291\reception_jobs\` (j22 … j40). Useful ones to copy: `j35_cable_and_wifi_read.py` (the cable and Wi-Fi from Windows' own records), `j33_setup_complete_check.py` (the whole setup), `j40_after_wifi_driver.py` (driver, scans, Windows Update, build).

**The Drive door (the usual road):** stage the stamped job and its `.sig`, copy both into a **fresh** folder under the cloud workspace's outputs, and commit **the job first, then the `.sig`, in two separate calls** to `H:\My Drive\Clinic Data Archive\ToReception\jobs\`. About two minutes later stage `H:\My Drive\Clinic Data Archive\FromReception\results\<stamped name>.out.txt` (a refusal is `<stamped name>.REFUSED.txt`). Those two folders are reached by the file-transfer tools only, not by the shell. A job that restarts the agent loses its own Drive result — read the next job instead.

**The server door:** from the assistant's browser, on any page of the clinic site, `fetch` a `POST` to `/finance/api/reception/jobs/submit` with JSON `{name, job (base64 of the stamped file), sig}`; read with `reception_sign.py read-token` → `POST /finance/api/reception/jobs/read`. Proven from the PC on 03-Oct (j13). Impractical for a job of several kilobytes — the Drive door is the working road. Full text: `deploy_kits/S453_RECEPTION_SERVER_DOOR/README.md`.

**Rules of both doors:** `.ps1`, `.cmd`, `.bat` or `.py`; first comment line `# timeout=NNN` changes the 600 s limit; a name is used once; older than 48 hours refused; runs as user `dell`, not elevated; ask for counts, never listings of patient folders. **A system change on that PC — a driver, a Windows setting — is the owner's hand at the PC, not a job.** **A rehearsal sends the installer's output to NUL and reads `install_log.txt`** (F-698).

**The S292 jobs, done at S294:** job folder `…\_EVIDENCE\S292\reception_jobs\` holds j41 (03-Oct, Windows' own currency, read only) and **j42 and j43, signed and served at 08:30–08:35 on Monday 05-Oct**: j42 put agent **S456.1** on that PC (`reception_agent.py` `d3d4b67b`, S453.1 kept as `.prev`), j43 read it back. The heartbeat now carries the Windows build and the last cumulative update. The next job number is j44.

## §1B · The Docterz reports — by the server road since S457 (F-697 closed)

Reception exports both reports each evening (the consultation report and the follow-up log); the reception PC posts them to the server by itself. On manojz the tracker's five-minute task now runs `docterz_fetch.py` first: it asks the server at most every 10 minutes, compares by md5 with what is already in `D:\Downloads`, fetches what is missing and checks it. The pickup then works as before. **Nothing is the owner's to run.**

- **Its record:** `D:\Downloads\DocterzArchive\_server_fetch_last.txt` (one line per pass).
- **Its switch:** a file `D:\Downloads\DocterzArchive\_server_fetch_OFF.txt` (or the PC-wide `D:\Downloads\margsync\_off\ALL_OFF.txt`).
- **It depends on manojz being awake.** A day reaches his portal the next time that PC is on.
- **The fallback, if the fetch is ever off** — his old line still works and is safe on any day (identical files are skipped by content):

```
cmd /c copy /y "H:\My Drive\Clinic Records\Docterz exports\*.csv" "D:\Downloads\"
```

- **Undo:** put `DOCTERZ_PICKUP.bat.bak_S457_90f45531` back in the tracker's folder. Full text: `deploy_kits/S457_DOCTERZ_SERVER_PATH/README.md`.

## §1D · The statement road (D674) — where things are since S293

- **The road:** bank → the owner's **personal** Gmail → `Bank_Statement_Relay.gs` (his personal Janitor project, trigger 07:00 daily, label `stmt-relayed`) → **Drive / Clinic Data Archive / Bank Statements / `<YYYY>`** (folder `1wGzaXnCoKcILw1VSv3UGYfQlVTl78UgT`; 2026 = `1-vrRDEZd0l53DKbZp3N51Laef5U759te`) → `stmt_shelf.py run` on the box at **05:40 and 07:30** (fetch + identify) → the packs page. Nothing passes through the clinic mailbox; nothing is mailed to the accountants until the owner presses *Send to accountants* (the pack).
- **The scripts:** repository copies in `deploy_kits/GAS_CURRENT/PersonalJanitor/Bank_Statement_Relay.gs` (S293 v2) and `…/UPIReconciliation/Bank_Statement_Filer.gs` (RETIRED; its trigger removed). The Janitor project is the owner's personal account's, shared to the clinic account as editor — the clinic browser pane edits it at `script.google.com/u/1/`; the clinic account's own `gas_export.py` drift check does not cover it.
- **If no statement reaches Drive:** his personal scripts' authorisation has lapsed again (F-723) — the failure mail goes to his personal inbox; ask him for the one re-authorisation (his hand). **Corrected at S294 (F-741):** the packs page's *re-read the shelf inbox* button only re-identifies files already on the shelf — it does **not** fetch; the fetch from Drive is the cron's alone (05:40 and 07:30), so a statement that reaches Drive after 07:30 is read the next morning.
- **The locked e-statements:** opened by the shelf with the per-account Yes Bank passwords he sets on the packs page (*Statement passwords*, six account rows then three bank-wide); the branch's unlocked copies, when they come, win (a locked twin becomes *duplicate of branch copy*).
- **The hand-over photos** (S472) are under `/root/finance/statements/packs/handover/<month>/` — in the encrypted nightly state bundle since S476.
- **The watch (S476):** the health page's row *Bank statements reaching Drive* — from the 3rd of a month, amber while no bank file was fetched this month and last month's cells are empty or partial. It measures the shelf's fetch, not the relay. **Read it at every close** (v17).
- **The readers:** `finance_yesbank.py` (the net-banking download), `yes_branch.py` (the branch's statement), `yes_monthly.py` (the e-mailed monthly statement, S479) — one row key across the three (F-744). The owner-only text of a shelf file: `/finance/packs/api/text/<id>` (S476), in his browser only.
- **Undo lines:** each kit's README (`S471_PACKS_SEPTEMBER`, `S475_YES_INTEREST_ROW`); the Filer's trigger is re-made by its `setupStmtFiler()` only by hand, on purpose.

What this section says of the console, the Today tile and the owner's own lines still holds; what it says of the staff's page, the single Turn on / Turn off and the 01-Sep floor for staff is superseded there.*

Under *Today's work*: the *Staff lists* line with **Turn on / Turn off**, then one block per list with **List** beside the name → `https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/aaj?as=<login>` (that person's list as they will see it; taps off).

The reading: `/root/finance/console_reading.dat` (+ `.lock`, `.err`, `console_build.log`) — derived, rebuilt when older than five minutes and asked for.
- **The switch and the taps:** setting `aaj.staff_on` = `<ISO time>|<login>` (empty = off) and `aaj.staff_first_on` (kept once); table `duty_tick` (one row per line and period; made on the first tap). **At the S294 close: off, no tap table.**

- **'late'** is the map's own rule: late on the day its allowed days are used up — the same on the list and on the console. A weekly tap line is late from the Tuesday, a monthly from the 4th.

## §1F · The salary pages and the System Board — where things are since S297

**The salary pages (D681, D682; kit S489).** The engine is `/root/staff_register/salary_policy.py` (v1.17-S489); it is served by `/root/staff_register/staff_register.py`, which this kit did not touch. The month's sheets:

```
https://followup.dr-manoj.in/register/salary
```

and a locked month's frozen sheets:

```
https://followup.dr-manoj.in/register/salary/locked?ym=2026-09
```

- **Sheet 2** has three tables: *this month's advances*, *instalment loans* (one line per person per loan; a group of advances that is one loan is named in `/root/staff_register/loan_pages.json` under *groups*), and one closing line per person.
- **A private loan** is named in `loan_pages.json` under *history* (months before the ledger began: skipped or paid — no figure). Its page opens at a balance **worked back from the ledger**; if the ledger's own adjustment for those months is not the one the record names, the page prints a *Check* note instead of a wrong opening.
- **A slip** is printed only for a person with a running instalment loan (`has_slip`).
- **`loan_pages.json` is made by the kit's `data_s489.py`** (`--write <path>`, `--check <path>`); change the record there and re-run, never by hand on the box.
- **The staff ledger** (`/root/staff_ledger.py`, data `/root/staff_ledger/ledger.jsonl`) is append-only. *Skip* adds a flat amount to a loan; *Defer* is free twice per financial year per person. **A skipped month is not yet one button** — his ruling is owed before the first skip (§2A item 4).
- **The late-charge settings are his** (`salary_policy_settings.json`, changed by him on 06-Oct; an audit file beside it). A sheet worked before a settings change will not match one worked after.
- **Nothing carries a negative net forward** — it is by hand until he asks for the panel.

## §1H · The POS total standing in for a missing bank statement — where things are since S300

**What it is (D691; kit S496).** When the bank's daily statement for a day has not come by the expected time, the staff may type that day's UPI total from the POS machine — one optional number, on the screen they already use. It counts only after the owner confirms it.

- **Where the staff see the box:** Sanjeevni — in Darpan's *Kal ka hisaab* form, in its own tinted box with its own button (Devanagari). The clinic — on the counter sheet and on the morning-match card. **It is offered only while that day's statement is not in and the expected time has passed.** NK Pathology has no daily page; the owner types its figure himself.
- **Where the owner acts:** a waiting figure is first on his approvals page, inside the *Kal ka hisaab* card; for the clinic and NK Pathology a folded line on *Clinic money*. Confirm · Reject; his own typing needs no second confirmation.

```
https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/approvals
```

```
https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/clinic/money
```

- **Code on the box:** `/root/finance/bank_standin.py` (the table `bank_standin`; `standing`, `type_figure`, `decide`, `sweep`, the owner's lines `pos_typed` · `pos_diff` · `pos_never`), read by `clinic_money.py` and `darpan_kal.py`; `bank_mpr_status.py` words the bank's line; `darpan_kal.html` and `finance_ui/finance_approvals.html` carry the screens. `finance_app.py` is not edited.
- **A row's life:** typed → confirmed or rejected; a new figure for the same day supersedes the old one; when the bank's statement arrives the row is closed as *replaced*, with the bank's figure and the difference — beyond the tolerance, one line for him; if the statement never comes, *lapsed*, one line. **A row is never deleted.**
- **The settings (on the screen, his):** `bank_mpr.expect_hhmm` — the bank's expected time on the next morning, default 10:00; `bank_standin.tolerance_p` — default ₹100; `bank_standin.on` — `0` hides the box from staff and leaves his own typing; `bank_standin.owner` — the one login that confirms.
- **What a day's reader gets:** `compute_day` (Sanjeevni) answers `online_source` (bank · pos · marg), `marg_online_p` and `standin`; the clinic's match answers a note within the tolerance and the flag `pos_diff` beyond it.
- **Not built:** words for the flag on the console (F-795); the *needs you* line's own wording for a waiting figure (F-794, the Sanjeevni chat's file); the morning match's verdict that names the bank while it is only awaited (F-796).
- **To take it back:** the first undo is his own setting — the box hidden from staff. The files: the kit's README carries its undo line — the five `.bak_S496_<first 8 of the old md5>` files copied back with `\cp`, `bank_standin.py` removed, `clinic-finance` restarted. The table stays; it is read by nothing then.

## §1J · The medical PC's pen-drive backup — where things are since S302

**What it is (D697; kit S499).** The pharmacy (medical) PC's agent `D:\SendToClinic\medical_agent.py` (S499.1 `d91fa79a…`) copies Marg's newest automatic backup set from `D:\MARGERP\serverbackup` to the stick, `E:\MargAuto_by_agent\<date_time>\`, every pass — only when the stick already holds a Marg backup; thirty sets kept; deletes only inside that folder.

- **What its heartbeat says:** the age of the hand-made `.mbk`, of the automatic copy on the stick, of the server-backup set, of the Drive copy; once a day the two zip directories compared. **LOUD** only when the stick has had no backup of any kind for three days, or has been absent or blank for a day.
- **Open (F-808):** the hand-made backup lists 34 members, the automatic 29 — the Marg engineer test-restores the automatic one; until then a hand-made backup onto the stick about weekly (the staff accept Marg's prompt on closing). A stale hand-made backup alone does not yet raise the loud line — one agent change after the hold.
- **How a new agent reaches that PC:** Drive `ToMedical\_kit\KIT_MANIFEST.txt` (`name | D:\SendToClinic\... | md5`, CRLF) delivers files by name into `D:\SendToClinic\` sub-folders — **never `medical_agent.py` itself**; a person double-clicks the installer from `D:\` (G: is not visible in his Remote Desktop session). The agent's switch: `D:\SendToClinic\_off\AGENT_OFF.txt`.
- **Retired, kept:** Drive `ToMedical\INSTALL_AGENT.bat` is a stub that says *retired*; the old bytes are `INSTALL_AGENT_v3_RETIRED_DO_NOT_RUN.txt`; S205.1 is `medical_agent_S205_1_RETIRED.py.txt`. **Still S205.1, to be brought up:** `PC_KITS\medical\kit.zip` and `verify_medical.py`'s words.
- **To take it back:** the kit's README — the switch on, `medical_agent.py.before_70d5c4e3` copied back, the switch off.

## §1L · The scan app since S306 (the Scan App project) — where things are

*The scan app is its own project since S305 (D689 item 3); its Book is `SCANAPP_SYSTEM_BOOK_v2_S306.md`, its backlog §2C below. §1C above (D664, S466) is history for the papers page.*

- **The scanner (S523, D711):** OpenCV in the phone's own browser — self-hosted at `https://followup.dr-manoj.in/scanapp/scan/opencv.js` (10 MB, gzip 3.4 MB, cached a year), **on for every phone by the one setting `scan.cv`** (the owner's tap on `/scanapp/admin`, 11-Oct). Full camera resolution, a live outline, four corners and the perspective warp, *Adjust* beside *Retake*; a phone that cannot carry the library keeps the old path by itself. The widget `/root/assetapp/scanner_widget.js` is **shared by seven surfaces** (two scan-app, five finance-app): every S523 / S531 change is inside branches only the scan app's intake sets. **The first real reading is Monday 12-Oct 08:00 IST** (a scheduled check: the 02:30 backup, smart-path scans against whole photos). The switch's page is too long for him (F-856): a short owner page later.
- **The papers page (S530, D710):** `https://followup.dr-manoj.in/scanapp/papers` — the three groups and **four sections** (Resumes · Miscellaneous documents · Construction work — counted on its own, never called clinic or MK by the system (D664 stands) · Personal — the owner only). **Sorted = accepted:** a paper that gets its group is accepted there and then (audit `d710_accepted`); the approve/reject door stays for refusing. The month page `/scanapp/papers/month` has a sections card.
- **Challans (S531, D689 item 2):** reception's intake has one big button, *📄 CHALLAN HAI?* → *✔ CHALLAN — bill baad mein*, reset after every Save. *Bills awaited*: `https://followup.dr-manoj.in/scanapp/papers/challans` — amber after 15 days (`d710.challan_amber_days`); a new bill of the same supplier within 60 days suggests its challan; join (S465's join plus the marks), *bill nahi aayega* closes, delete after the join moves the file to `_deleted_challans`. A pharmacy challan is handed to the Sanjeevni side as `kind='Challan'`, `doc_kind='challan'`, `status='captured'` — its register does not see it as a bill to pair until it chooses to read challans.

## §2N · The NK Pathology chat's backlog — written at its S356 close (28-Oct-2026, evening)

*This project's first Runbook section (until S356 its backlog lived only in its entry points and its project document `claude/S351_NK_PC_SPREADSHEETS_ROUTING.md`). Owns: `/root/labdata/`, `/root/finance/lab_door.py`, the lab PC's agent (`deploy_kits/S655_LAB_PC_AGENT`), Drive `FromLab` / `ToLab`, the DTH laptop. `finance_app.py` and `pc_kits.py` are the parent's — a PLANNED line first.*

**Where things are.** The lab PC (LABPC, 192.168.1.33, Labmate 9.08 in `E:\Labmate982`) runs `C:\ClinicLabAgent\lab_agent.py` S655.1 at logon (~08:30–12:30): a heartbeat every 5 min to Drive `Clinic Data Archive\FromLab\heartbeat.txt` and the server; `lab.mdb` + `Captdata.mdb` zipped to the server ~5 min after switch-on and every 3 h (`/root/labdata/incoming/labpc_YYYYMMDD_HHMM.zip`, 30 days). Jobs: sign on manojz with `python3 -B D:\dr-manoj-git\drmanoj-clinic-automation\deploy_kits\S655_LAB_PC_AGENT\manojz\lab_sign.py sign D:\Downloads\margsync\_config\lab_agent_key.txt <job>` and put both files into Drive `ToLab\jobs\` (the Drive connector can write there; the server relay is not reachable from the manojz VM — proxy 403); results come back in `FromLab\results\` in ~2–5 min (Drive sync both ways). A running job is NOT stopped by `_off\JOBS_OFF.txt`; it ends at its own `# timeout=`.

**The day report (base layout of the day page).** Labmate's "PATIENT REGISTER WITH DETAILS (All) — Receipt amount till date" for one day: Sr., Name (age/sex), Ref. by, Chrg., Disc., Net, Rect., Ref., Bal.; receipt lines under a bill; the Day Total; the summary (fully paid / due / refund / free / credit / cancel …); "Receipt Status": CASH (n) and UPI (n). 27-Oct's copy: `C:\Users\DR MANOJ AGARWAL\Desktop\LABMATE.pdf` on LABPC (staff Sukhveer, 18:57), and Drive `FromLab\daypdf_S356b\01_20261027_185707_LABMATE.pdf` (patient names — never into the repository or a project document).

**The backlog, in order:**
1. **The morning day page — mock-up first** (D784 §7A). From the newest `labpc_*.zip`: mdbtools on the server (one `apt install mdbtools` line for the owner), a nightly SQLite read copy, then yesterday's page in the report's layout — split DTH classes (DTH OPD, IPD, Ayushman, Cashless) · referring doctors · SELF walk-ins; gross / discount / net; cash and UPI against the ICICI POS feed already on the server; DTH bills with IPD / receipt no. ("other ref no."); the error list — the seven points S356 found on 27-Oct are its first checks (export before closing; a SELF bill with no receipt; same-name same-day pair; title/sex mismatch; stray characters in a name; no age; discount by referrer). Mock-up shown to the owner before any install.
2. **Small kit** — the lab heartbeat's reception-only lines (Docterz exports, Chrome download folder, X-ray inbox) switched off in the lab agent's `config.json` (the attention line goes); `lab_door.health_row` into the health page (ONE line in the parent's `finance_app.py` — PLANNED line first).
3. **The DTH laptop's agent** (the same kit, a second key) once the lab PC has run a clean week (from 26-Oct → after 2-Nov).
4. **The slip number in Labmate's booking-slip field** (§9 D), then the packages finder (§9 C), then the report composer (§9 B, Dr Bhawna's say first).
5. **His open questions** (project document §6): did DTH pay the phantom ₹550 lines; the DTH / LAB split of the old statement format; the overhead percentages; whether DTH needs its receipt number; the day export taken at closing.

**Rules learned here.** A lab-PC job that copies files lists first, checks the extension itself and caps the count (F-1044). `cmd dir` takes the pattern inside the path (`dir "E:\*.pdf" /s /b`). `lab_sign.py` with `-B` (F-1041). A text file in a kit is LF unless `.gitattributes` pins it CRLF (F-1026). Patient files stay in the clinic's own Drive and the device VM, never in the cloud workspace.

## §4 · Rules at the boundary

**Earned at S323 (the Scan App project):** **a figure entered by hand on a form that holds less than it shows is a fault of the form** — a date field that keeps month and year resets the day on every save (F-976); **a status word is computed for the past as well as the future** — "OK" for a warranty already over is false (F-977); **every record that takes a paper has a door to link one later** (F-978); **a match takes the item's own unit, and a quantity equal to the pack size is asked about** (F-979); **a back-dated paper's month follows its own date when the reading has one** (F-980); a data step that writes history writes it before the opening count, marked and undoable, one stamp per paper (S623 R1).

**Earned at S331 (the parent):** **a statement kit's walk runs its chain check on scratch copies of the live store, and a failure prints the rows behind it on the owner's screen** (F-965: the made-up half was green; only the live half found Darpan's loan cut before its date) · **what a figure is made of is said beside it** — a *remaining after* line names the advances in it and when each is cut (F-966, F-967) · **interest is never counted as advance repaid** (F-964) · **the hand-over check of `.gitignore` covers every unpublished kit folder, any chat's** (F-971) · **a vendor's refusal is narrowed by test before a theory reaches the owner** — the Hindi theory went out first and was wrong (F-968).

**Earned at S334 (the Sanjeevni chat):** **a brief is read back on the PC before the line is given — its first line names the version and its md5 is the one the chat wrote; a file is sent under a name never used before in the session** (F-963: the reviewed v2 was sent under the v1 name and the transfer wrote the earlier copy; the runner built v1); what staff "saw happen by itself" is read from the store's own record of who did what and when before it is explained; a hand-over to a phone is once (F-702); one tap never sends to everyone (F-961); a system list never rides on a person's list unasked (D749).

**Earned at S332 (the Sanjeevni chat):** what a reading is FOR is asked before its reader is chosen — every live use of a pharmacy scan was the header (D747); a kit on another chat's file carries a PLANNED line, a board message to that chat and that chat's own walk chain (S601); a page rendered in the browser is proved on its API and its text, never by a page dump, and a door that opens a fresh handle is closed by every caller (S602 R1); a kit's lock is removed by the command the permission list allows, named in the rulebook, never left for the owner (F-950).

**Earned at S330 (the Sanjeevni chat):** a stored reading is credited to a reader only after the code that wrote that table for that lane is read (F-949); a claim about what a Marg export carries is made from the raw file, never from memory of its columns (11:56, 12:01); a date shown to a staff member is one he can act on (D746).

**Earned at S324 (the Sanjeevni chat):** a rule that stands in for a fact the system cannot see is relaxed only with a replacement test computed on the store (F-926, F-929); a tie that says an order arrived says nothing about which lines — units added on it need an end (F-930); a page whose read writes is not opened by a screen reader (F-925); a big brief is read until a read finds nothing, and a design a read finds not computable leaves the kit (F-929); a time on the board is read, never estimated (F-934).

**Earned at S322 (the Sanjeevni chat):** a line about a report names its day and why what landed is not enough (F-910); a link that one reader follows and three do not is a second source — one reader, one record (F-911); a page check asserts on the element, not on a word (F-912); a design the owner reshapes three times in half an hour is re-mocked each time before a line of code, and the build waits for his word on the last one.

**Earned at S318 (the Scan App project):** a run cut off by a restart is resumed by a brief that first proves nothing live moved, never re-run blind (F-908); an "unchanged" check compares program files by hash, never a listing a timer writes into (F-909); a staff screen follows his mock-up line by line and is installed off (S568 K1); a switch that hides a page from staff also hides every offer of it elsewhere (S571 K1); a paper hidden from a viewer is hidden in every list that mentions it, its amount and link included (S571 K1 note); a page offered to link a paper never changes the paper (D664); an old thing he owns with only a date is recordable without inventing a bill (S575).

**Earned at S311:** a source is chosen by whether it reaches the output's day (F-884); a count over a folder covers every name it can hold before a question goes to him (F-883); a line for the owner is marked where it is made and every road out is walked for it (F-885); net at the bill, never the day, and give the walk a made-up row that tells them apart (F-886); what he wants kept aside goes to one place he sees and nowhere else until his word (D717); "later" from him is a park — not raised again.

**Earned at S305:** a gap between two figures is explained from the rows behind both (F-870); a phone feature is proved on a phone, and a forward is changed with a temporary redirect (F-871); a door the owner wants closed "only if safe" is closed in two steps — record first, refuse only on a week's evidence (D713, S535); a page's duplicate prompt that counts money twice is a finding even when each mail is real (F-868); the proof of an online booking is the line in Docterz, not a mailbox (D714); a second chat's reminder for the same hour is retired at the close so one chat acts (S305).

**Earned at S306 (the Scan App project):** a finding about a scanner is measured on the stored scans before a line of code (F-842 — 33 of 40 whole photos, read from the backup); a library in the browser beats an app to install when the flow must stay one tap (D711); a switch the owner taps is said to him with what it reaches (F-856); a paper sorted by a person is accepted by that act, not by a second tap (D710); the system places a paper only on a *person's* earlier placements and never feeds itself (S532); a supplier seen on two lanes decides nothing; the lab and Dr MK lanes are never moved by the system; a shared file (`scanner_widget.js`, seven surfaces) is changed only inside the branch the one caller sets, with a PLANNED line first and the other callers walked unchanged (D646); a kit's `.json` is renamed before it meets `.gitignore` (the F-300 family, a fourth time); a `pkill -f` pattern never rides the tool shell's own command line. *(The text below is v225's, whole.)*

**Earned at S304:** a correction is part of the build and heals the data it governs (D704, `BUILD_NORM_v1`); a feed into a shared sheet decides by what a message is (F-834); a job a person ran once is not a pipeline (F-835); an alert day and a deadline are two fields (F-775); every kit number is claimed on the board before its folder is named — every kit (F-831); a `.gitignore` check is last-match-wins (F-832); no git in the device shell, read-only included (F-833); when his view and the data disagree, show the data in a line and set a check — do not argue (F-837); a file at drift 4 is read whole before its fifth patch, and the read pays for itself (F-836); a page carrying a key shows it only to him, and the key is never printed anywhere else.

**Earned at S302:** a backup is proven by a restore, not a copy (F-808); an alarm's threshold is derived from the rule it watches (F-806); a no-show is judged only on data that reaches the day (F-811); the number gate in the device shell runs with `SANJ_CONFIG_STORE` set (F-810); when his Remote Desktop cannot see a drive, the delivery goes to `D:\` by the agent's own channel and the double-click stays his; a desktop shortcut to a Drive folder points at Explorer (F-809); during his hold, an install he asks for and a staff-reported repair are not new builds. *(The text below is v220's, whole.)*

**Earned at S300:** a time is read from a clock or it is not written (F-791); a file that exists in one place is copied before it is edited (F-792); a read does not write (F-793); when he changes what he asked for in the middle of a build, the build stops and he is shown the new thing first — a mock-up — before another line is written; when he says *final*, the mock-up as it stands is the specification; a build he parks is kept in memory and in the pending list and is not raised again; a key — a topic name is one — is never in a repository, a kit, a page or a chat line. *(The text below is v218's, whole.)*

**Earned at S298:** when he edits a mock-up himself, the build follows his edit, line for line; a question that asks for yes or no gets the one word; a panel that saves itself is looked at by reads only; an open door is described in public only after it is shut — until then its detail stays on the PC; a kit is walked in the server's time zone and from the place it is packed; a kit of any size is read by a fresh agent until a read finds nothing. *(The text below is v216's, whole.)*

**Earned at S297:** his approvals are few and arrive together — finish what does not prompt, then ask once; no board line mid-session unless a number is taken or a live file is protected; what he types on his board is answered on his board at the next close; a ticked line is gone from his list, not struck through; a list for him is short — five lines, one sentence each; a salary figure never enters the repository; a preview of money pages says what it was worked on; what he has closed (*"nothing more required"*) is not raised again. *(The text below is v215's, whole.)*

The owner's standing rulings (restated in START_HERE §0). **Earned at S294:** when he stops a build, it stops at once and he is shown what he asked about, in one line; **a new screen for staff is installed switched off and shown to him first** — he turns it on; **nothing stale is put in front of a person as theirs** (D679's floor); a figure for him is read on the page he will read it on; a refused publish is cleared in minutes and said plainly as the assistant's miss. Carried from v212: analysis first when he says so, then *"take my yes"* — the whole plan, no second round; a statement never passes through the staff-readable mailbox; the accountants get the pack, not the mail; a yes/no question gets one word; an explanation is short and in his words; a category he has not finalised is not guessed; credentials, sign-ins, passwords on his pages and Apps Script authorisations are his hands; reception-PC work at 08:30.

**Earned at S319:** an outage is read from the machine's own record, and that record is made to survive a restart first (F-919); an alert that matters goes by two roads, and a shared free service is retried until it takes the message (F-917); Docterz is the record for a name, and a first-seen copy is corrected where it is read (F-918); a helper's name is checked against the whole file before it is added — a later definition with the same name silently wins (S576's walk caught one); an account-access grant (an OAuth consent) is the owner's hand even when he says yes in chat — the assistant's browser refuses it; a large project-knowledge document read only to prove a survivor costs its whole size in tokens — prove survivors by the repository's gate instead; a D or F number is taken on the board the moment it is minted, never held for the close, and the canon folder is re-read just before a close assembles (F-920).


**Earned at S325:** a void closes every door the record had, not only its row (F-922); a walk on a copy of live data finds its own rows by its own mark (F-923); a file time is not proof of the bytes — a fresh staged path and a read-back by hash before the publish (F-924); a money flow is tested as a dummy record walked as the owner, end to end, then removed (his word, 12:17 18-Oct); a number is taken the moment it is minted — S325 held four for hours (F-920 again).

**Earned at S327 (the Sanjeevni chat):** a rule relaxed is a rule verified elsewhere — a start-of-day closing is used only once a per-key test, with the morning's known Marg work excluded by cause, has accepted it (D740, R2-B2); cover comes from the export and what followed it, not from the visit (R2-B4); a staff page's display order is not its step identity — find every place that does arithmetic on the ids before re-ordering (R1-B1 of S587); a gate between two staff members is weighed against D712 and the real mornings before it is designed; a kit that adds a settings row refreshes the stored watch or says when the row appears; every brief gets one independent read before the build — both of this session's carried three blockers each.

**Earned at S326:** a check judges the whole line; only the display is cut (F-937); a kit that must reach a database the box already reaches another way copies the way that worked, not the way that was fixed on paper — and its rehearsal includes a stand-in that refuses the first road (F-938); a form test is proven by the site's own answer and the mail's arrival, never by a click (F-939); a contact form's recipients are read in the mail itself, not assumed from the site's settings (F-940); a vendor's access is closed by listing every door — FTP, SSH keys, panel users, site administrators — and asking him whose each is (F-941).

**Earned at S328 (the Sanjeevni chat):** a spine handle without `.ok` makes `stock_figure` answer "no spine" quietly and the engine fall back to the tables — name the class and assert `engine == "spine"` (S593 R1-B1); a quantity has a unit before it has a column — the spine's units are not an order line's packs (R1-B2); the 20-letter key is identity for stock, not for arrivals — a bill line answers the order line of its own spelling, once (F-942); a rule's record and a rule's owner differ — a pharmacy kit may add the parent's crontab line when its README says so (S594); an install-day state is named for the owner before the build, not discovered after (S598 R1-B2); a read-only entry point tells the judge not to judge (F-943); one independent read per brief — six, two and two blockers caught before three builds.

**Earned at S329:** a binary travels as base64 text with its MD5 checked where it lands (F-944); a vendored library with number tables rides as base64, never as a gate exemption (F-945); a send road is proved on his own phone, type by type, before the screen that uses it is built (S595); when a provider answers one key the way it answers a made-up one, the fault is the provider's account, not the key — escalate with the request ids and change nothing (F-946); a build that needs a credential checks it by a read first and stops cleanly if refused (S599); no git in the device shell, read-only included (F-947).

**Earned at the S329 post-close:** a provider that answers our key as it answers a made-up one is given the evidence — request ids, a control, the panel read without a change — and nothing on our side is altered to work around it; when they ask for a raw test, it is one read-only line whose output carries no key (F-946); a panel *Regenerate* is never pressed on a guess.

**Earned at S333 (the Sanjeevni chat):** a reminder clears on a fact the server received after the moment the staff member's own action made it due, never on a tap (F-952); a past day's report is read from its receipt, never from the live set the next export supersedes (S603 R1-B2); a read path says it is read-only (F-953); before saying where a vendor is paid from, compute the route today — and when the owner says a page is wrong, read it live in the browser first (F-954); a report's returns are decided by the bill's own sign (F-956); a gate built on a function's output is a layer on it, never called from inside it (S604 R1-B1); the API-credit runner can carry a two-machine kit unattended when every wait is bounded and every path full (S604).


**Earned at S335 (the Sanjeevni chat):** a promise about another system's behaviour is read in that system's own export first (F-985 — Marg drops a line that did not come); a quality check is proved on real outcomes before it refuses a person, and where a device cannot tell, the server lists and a person decides (S618 R1); a scan's reading is a note and Marg is the count (S619 R1); one reader per question before the screens (F-984); two code maps by sub-agents, then the brief, then one independent read — each of this session's three briefs had 5–8 blockers only the read against the code and data found.
